Rebuilt as validated, moderated forms: contact, event submission, meeting RFP, wedding enquiry, partner listing request, visitors-guide request, newsletter (see `src/lib/forms.ts`).

## Interactive features & third-party integrations found

| Feature | Where | Rebuilt as |
|---|---|---|
| Simpleview CRM listings directory (filters, map, paging) | 48 directory pages | Server-rendered filterable directory + map (`Directory.tsx`) |
| Events calendar (Simpleview) | /events/ | Events browser + month calendar + .ics export |
| Weather widget (weather-icons, provider via Simpleview plugin) | Homepage, /plan-your-visit/weather/ | NWS-powered weather centre |

> Known limitation: the live widget also matches listings by **secondary** CRM categories, which are not exposed in public page markup. Directory membership in the rebuild uses each listing's primary category plus preset amenity filters. A Simpleview CRM export (listing → all categories) will close this gap during final migration.
